#  NBA Best Bets: Player Prop Bet Picks (Wednesday, 11/11/26) 
[ See RotoBaller at the top of Google ](https://google.com/preferences/source?q=rotoballer.com) by [Dan Palyo](https://www.rotoballer.com/author/thunderdan "Posts by Dan Palyo")

![Karl-Anthony Towns - Fantasy Basketball Rankings, NBA DFS Lineup Picks](https://www.rotoballer.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/05/Karl-Anthony-Towns-Fantasy-Basketball-Rankings-NBA-DFS-icon-rotoballer-1220x852.jpg) Nov 11, 2026, 9:47 AM ET
NBA player prop bets and statistical analysis for Wednesday, November 11. Matchup advantages, projection edges, and value lines for tonight's slate.
Blowouts have been killing some of my favorite bets lately! Last night I faded some spots due to projected blowouts and missed out on some easy prop wins, while then targeting the Clippers-Suns game with several that ultimately fell short when the Clippers (who were only 4-point underdogs) lost by a million!
For today's picks, I'll include the best odds available at one of the books I use, but I'll also include the price at [Novig](https://novig.onelink.me/JHQQ/nfg364h9), which is one of our partners and absolutely one of the best sports prediction markets out there.
As always, follow [RotoBaller on X](https://x.com/RotoBaller) and download the [RotoBaller app](https://www.rotoballer.com/app) for all your fantasy football and betting needs. Now, here are some of my favorite NBA bets and player props for Wednesday's NBA slate!
_**Featured Promo:** New Novig users get a $25 purchase match (50% discount up to $25) on your first Novig deposit, and [6 free months of RotoBaller's "Big-4" Premium Pass](https://www.rotoballer.com/no) (NFL, NBA, MLB, NHL) which includes exclusive tools for Betting, Props, DFS and more!_ [CLAIM IT NOW](https://www.rotoballer.com/no)
## NBA Player Prop Bets
We have a bit of a theme today (not planned, I promise) with a lot of big men popping in great rebounding spots!
**[Karl-Anthony Towns](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20001431/Karl-Anthony+Towns) OVER 12.5 Rebounds (-111 Novig)**
Towns leads all qualified players in the NBA with a 33.4% defensive rebounding rate. He's averaging 13.8 boards per game (in 31 minutes per contest) and facing a Cavs team that is allowing the second-most rebounds to opposing centers this season. Of the four games where he's gone under 12.5 boards, three of them have been on the hook right at 12, and several of those were games where he played low minutes due to foul trouble or a blowout.
The Knicks big man just faced Cleveland a few games ago and snagged 13 boards in that one. His elite rebounding rate, paired with his potential to play his full share of minutes tonight in what should be a close game (you better show up, Cavs!) as Cleveland is only a 4-point underdog on the road in New York.
**[Walker Kessler](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002889/Walker+Kessler) OVER 10.5 Rebounds (-130 Novig)**
Towns has the second-best matchup for boards tonight, while Kessler has the absolute smash spot for bigs. We saw Towns clobber the Bucks last night for 16 rebounds in only 27 minutes! Milwaukee is dead last in rebounds allowed to centers, and also is allowing the most offensive rebounds to opponents in the league.
Enter Kessler, who averages 3.5 offensive rebounds per game, putting him inside the top ten in the NBA. He's been feasting on the glass lately, going for 11 or more boards in five of his last seven games.
We have a slight blowout risk here as the Lakers are 15-point favorites and the Bucks are playing their second game in as many nights, but I am not sure that is going to matter for Kessler as we saw Towns go nuts without having to close the game.
**[Anthony Davis](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20000468/Anthony+Davis) OVER 22.5 Points (-115 Novig)**
AD has been in excellent form this year when healthy and in the lineup. He's giving the Wizards exactly what they had hoped for when they made the trade for him last season. His usage rate is sitting right around 30%, and he's gone over this points total in six of the nine games in which he's appeared so far this year. He's averaging 25.7 points on the season and coming off a massive 44-point outing against a good defensive team in Detroit.
This game looks juicy for props, as it carries a 227.5-point total and only a 2.5-point spread. The Pacers' defense has been rough this year, and we saw them allow both [Kyrie Irving](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20000574/Kyrie+Irving) and [Cooper Flagg](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003444/Cooper+Flagg) to have huge nights on Monday. Look for AD to crush this prop, and I'd consider laddering him up to 30 points tonight.

## NBA Value Player Props
**[Day'Ron Sharpe](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002697/Day'Ron+Sharpe) OVER 11.5 Rebounds + Assists (-125 Novig)**
I might lose some of you here. I mean, how can we trust Sharpe to get enough minutes tonight when his playing time has been inconsistent, and he keeps getting in foul trouble?
I will tell you why! The Pelicans are too soft a matchup to pass up! They're bleeding production to centers, allowing the sixth-most rebounds and third-most assists per game to opposing fives.
Sharpe's game log is all over the map, I know. But he's been dishing out dimes at an elite rate lately with 27 assists over his last three games. If we set the bar at 24 minutes, Sharpe has gone over this number in three of the four games that he's played that much this season, with 11 right on the nose against Charlotte.
He's a stat-stuffing machine, and I am taking the leap of faith on his ability to stay on the court tonight. If he does, he'll smash this number.
**[Alexandre Sarr](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003215/Alexandre+Sarr) OVER 6.5 Rebounds (-110 Fanatics)**
_Compare at Novig at -111_
It's been a weird start to the season for Sarr, as he's been asked to share the frontcourt with Anthony Davis and take a smaller role on offense. However, his rebounding has been superb lately. He's averaging 9.4 boards over his last five games and has gone for 8 or more rebounds in each contest.
The Pacers are getting drubbed by opposing centers on the boards. They are allowing the fifth-most rebounds to centers, but have been particularly bad of late. Before the Dallas game, they had allowed eight straight centers to snag 9 or more boards. Now I know some of those games they played small and were without [Ivica Zubac](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20001694/Ivica+Zubac), but Zubac has been back for a few games now, and the rebounding numbers for opponents have not abated.
Again, I expect this to be a close game, so I think we get something like 26-28 minutes from Sarr tonight and that he'll crush this number in a good spot.
**[Cedric Coward](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003477/Cedric+Coward) OVER 9.5 Rebounds + Assists (-105 DraftKings) **
One of the reasons that Memphis is off to such a great start has to be the stellar play of their second-year wing, Cedric Coward. He doesn't have to score the ball much to make a big impact, as his defense, rebounding, and passing have all been quite good and are helping to elevate his teammates.
He's averaging 11 rebounds and assists per game this season, with most of that production coming from his strong rebounding (8.4 boards per game). However, he also has five games with three or more assists this season, and he can help facilitate on offense since the Grizzlies really don't have a traditional pass-first point guard in the starting lineup. [Ty Jerome](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002307/Ty+Jerome) and [Cameron Boozer](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003709/Cameron+Boozer) both chip in assists, but Coward can also be a play-maker as well.
I love this game tonight as the Grizzlies will be tested by the Denver Nuggets at home. The Grizzlies are 3.5-point underdogs, and I think they'll battle with Denver the entire way. A competitive game means that there's a good chance Coward gets the 30 minutes we are looking for in order to him to hit his over.
Good luck if you're tailing, and thanks for reading!

#  DraftKings King of the Court: Favorites and Sleeper Picks (11/10/26) 
[ See RotoBaller at the top of Google ](https://google.com/preferences/source?q=rotoballer.com) by [Koby Hanson](https://www.rotoballer.com/author/singedpancakes "Posts by Koby Hanson")

Koby Hanson provides his favorite players to target with prop bets tonight (Tuesday, 11/10) in DraftKings King of the Court contest. Turn five bucks into a big payout by finding the right player to bet on every week this season!
Finally, a King Of The Court slate without [Luka Doncic](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20001984/Luka+Don%C4%8Di%C4%87)[!](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20001984/Luka+Don%C4%8Di%C4%87) We also don't have to worry about [Victor Wembanyama](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003031/Victor+Wembanyama) either, who is having an incredible start to his season as well. It's nice to see the bets get spread out a little more, which gives us a chance to focus on more options around the league rather than the chalk option just taking the cake like he did multiple times last year and already once this year. We didn't have KOTC last week due to Election Day, which tells me this is going to be a Tuesday promo every week. Hopefully we can get off to a great start and smack one here.
With plenty of options, I'll narrow it down to five spots, including two of the more popular options on the slate, plus a few pivots I'm considering. If you are looking for any other bets or DFS help, make sure to check out the RotoBaller Premium Discord where I and other writers will be in there chatting about the upcoming slate and any updates that might pop up along the way!
In this article, I will provide my favorite picks for the **DraftKings King of the Court contest on** **Tuesday, November 10th.** And don't forget to grab a [RotoBaller Premium Pass](https://www.rotoballer.com/all-premium-access-nfl-mlb-nba-nhl), which is highly discounted and includes DFS, betting, props, and season-long tools like the Team Sync platform.

## How King of the Court Works
King of the Court has quickly become very popular amongst DraftKings users. It involves picking who you believe will be the player with the highest PRA (Points/Rebounds/Assists) total for the night. You don't even have to win the prop bet to win the KOTC! You simply have to make a qualifying wager of $5 or more on the player who finishes as the leader in PRA.
There are a few ways to go about it; a lot of people will take the chalk and take the small bonus bets they get with it (since you have to split the pot with hundreds of thousands of other bettors). Or if you are like me, you like to get a little different, and that can lead to some big payouts!
I hit a few of these last season, including the game where [Bam Adebayo](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20001836/Bam+Adebayo) went for 83 points! That led to me landing over 1k in bonus bets!
[![](data:image/svg+xml,<svg%20xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"%20viewBox="0%200%20600%20630"></svg>)](https://www.rotoballer.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/KOTC-e1792576683574.png)
## DraftKings King of the Court Picks: The Chalk
All odds are current as of 7:00 AM and courtesy of DraftKings Sportsbook.
**[Giannis Antetokounmpo](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20000497/Giannis+Antetokounmpo) over 42.5 Points and Assists (-122)**
This is our replacement for Luka this week. Giannis Antetokounmpo has been on a tear this year, even though the Heat haven't been the powerhouse most thought they would be so far.
Tonight, they get a decent matchup against a pretty poor Utah Jazz team. The Jazz are scrappy and keep games close, but haven't quite figured out a way to finish the job. The Heat are 10-point favorites in this one, and they have the highest implied team total on the slate.
Giannis is averaging just over 45 PRA a game this year, and has four games of 49 PRA or more. The Jazz have been solid against forwards to start the season, but the best power forward they have faced has been Zion, and he dropped 23 points on them in both meetings. They've been forced to play a lot smaller this year with [Jaren Jackson Jr.](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20001991/Jaren+Jackson+Jr.) and [Jusuf Nurkic](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20000827/Jusuf+Nurki%C4%87) out. [Lauri Markkanen](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20001831/Lauri+Markkanen) is also questionable for this one, while Utah's top scorer, [Keyonte George](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003044/Keyonte+George), has already been ruled out.
Giannis likely runs through this team, and if the Jazz can keep it close, I'd be surprised if Giannis didn't finish with close to 50. But if the Heat win too easily, perhaps Giannis doesn't play his full allotment of minutes, and that's why we have pivots!
## DraftKings King of the Court Picks: The Pivots
When you are going after players such as Luka or Giannis, you definitely have a higher probability of hitting, but everyone else is taking them, too. It makes it likely that the payout will be quite minimal. So having other options in great spots, and hoping they pop off while guys like Luka maybe have a bit of a low-key game, is ideal when trying to go for as big of bonus bets as possible!
**[Karl-Anthony Towns](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20001431/Karl-Anthony+Towns) over 12.5 Rebounds (-130)**
The big thing we have to worry about here is the blowout potential. This game has the highest spread on the slate, with the Knicks as massive 15.5-point favorites. However, the Bucks have only had two games this season where they lost by 25 points or more, so they have kept games relatively close.
The big thing I want to focus on here is the center position. The Bucks haven't been able to stop centers all season long, allowing the fifth-most points and rebounds.
KAT is averaging nearly 14 rebounds a game, and if he can continue to up the scoring, he's going to be a serious threat to finish as the slate leader in PRA. Towns is averaging 1.19 PRA/min this season, which trails only Giannis and [Jayson Tatum](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20001829/Jayson+Tatum) on this slate.
KAT is in one of the best matchups on the slate, and the Bucks are going to have a hard time stopping him with a frontcourt that consists of [Myles Turner](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20001424/Myles+Turner), who looks disinterested in playing basketball this season.
**Jayson Tatum over 33.5 Points and Assists (-116)**
There are certainly some fun storylines surrounding this game with [Jaylen Brown](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20001672/Jaylen+Brown) and [Paul George](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20000742/Paul+George) facing the teams that traded them for each other this offseason. But more than anything, it's a really great matchup between two Eastern Conference contenders and one of the few games on this slate that is guaranteed to be competitive until the final seconds.
The 218-point total is the lowest on the board, but the level of competition here is sure to bring out some ceiling performances from a few of the superstars involved.
While Jaylen Brown has to share usage with three other stars in Philly, Jayson Tatum is the clear alpha dog in Boston. His 35.1% usage rate trails only Luka Doncic and Giannis Antetokounmpo on this slate. He also has the second-best PRA/min rate (behind Giannis) at 1.21.
Tatum has played fewer than 30 minutes in Boston's last two games, which turned into blowouts. But I think we'll see him play minutes in the mid-to-upper 30s tonight in this spotlight game. He leads the team in assists, averaging over seven per game, and while his rebounds are down a bit this year, we know he has double-digit upside in boards. In fact, he has 10.4 boards per game against the Sixers over his last ten meetings with them.
**[Shai Gilgeous-Alexander](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20001999/Shai+Gilgeous-Alexander) over 38.5 Points and Assists (-106)**
I don't know that I can really consider this a sneaky pivot, but I would be doing everyone a disservice not putting SGA up here. This OKC Thunder team has stayed competitive even while down a few guys, and this matchup is solid. This should be one of the closer games on the slate, and with SGA locked into a huge usage role right now, it's going to be hard to avoid. The Blazers have some solid guys on defense, but have struggled against the guard position.
Luka Doncic went for 53, [Devin Booker](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20001460/Devin+Booker) for 33, and [Jamal Murray](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20001666/Jamal+Murray) for 35, all against Portland within the last four games. The rebounds and assists have been a bit lackluster this year for SGA, which hurts his chances a bit and may have people going to other spots. But he is averaging about eight combined rebounds and assists a game this season. If he can drop close to 40 points again, he is going to be right up there with the rest of them.
**Devin Booker over 26.5 Points (-111)**
For the final spot, I'm rolling with a guy who is on a heater in the scoring department lately. As I mentioned, he had 33 against Portland not too long ago. He also had 30 points against Orlando and scored 32 in just 29 minutes last night against the Spurs in a blowout. Booker has four games of 30+ points, and his best game was 43-7-7 against the Warriors.
Now he gets another great matchup against the Clippers, in what should be a close matchup with a high-scoring game.
The Clippers have been giving up the fifth most points to opposing PGs this season, and big games have come from [Jeremiah Fears](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003448/Jeremiah+Fears) and [Immanuel Quickley](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002550/Immanuel+Quickley) most recently. If those guys can do it, just imagine what Booker can do to this team. Booker hasn't done much in terms of rebounds this season, which is why he will be looked at as a solid pivot option. He is also seeing close to 35 minutes a game.
Booker will continue being the primary scorer until the likes of [Jalen Green](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002668/Jalen+Green) or [Miles Bridges](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002003/Miles+Bridges) step up a bit more, but for now, expect 30+ from Booker in points alone.

## What is Novig?
Novig is one of the fastest-growing peer-to-peer sports prediction markets. On Novig, users trade directly with each other without the "vig" that is associated with a typical sportsbook.
The vig represents the money that sportsbooks charge you to use their service. It's a hidden cost built into the odds offered on each side of a bet. When a book offers -110 odds on both sides of a bet, they're keeping close to 10% of every bettor's wager, regardless of whether you win or lose.
On [Novig](https://novig.onelink.me/JHQQ/nfg364h9), users trade with each other on the outcomes of sporting events, similarly to the way that investors trade on the stock market. But instead of having to beat the books, you just have to win against other users - there's no commission kept by the site.
Users can bet on most traditional sports markets, including moneyline bets, spreads, totals, player props, parlays, and futures! No politics, no betting on wars...just sports!
## NBA Prop Bet Picks
_All odds are from Novig and were accurate as of 6:00 AM ET. All odds listed are from Novig and shown as percentages, not traditional American odds._
**[Cooper Flagg](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003444/Cooper+Flagg) OVER 14.5 Rebounds + Assists (50%)**
We are watching the birth of a superstar in Dallas. Flagg's sophomore season has been every bit as good as advertised, as he's averaging 24-7-7 through his first nine games and has helped lead the Mavericks to a 6-4 start.
Flagg was fantastic last night in the Mavericks' romp of the Nets, going 25-5-12 in only 28 minutes. He didn't have to expend any energy in the fourth quarter and should be relatively fresh for this matchup against the Pacers tonight.
The last time Flagg faced the Pacers, he got hurt and was unable to finish the game. He had five boards and six assists in that one...in only 17 minutes. It's never this easy, but if you wanna double that and project him for 34 minutes tonight, then he's a triple-double candidate and he could easily smash this prop.
We could see [Kyrie Irving](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20000574/Kyrie+Irving) rested, too, which would push Flagg even more into a point-forward role. But even if Kyrie plays, I love Flagg's upside in what projects to be a closer game than we saw Dallas in last night.
**[Stephon Castle](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003192/Stephon+Castle) OVER 20.5 Points + Rebounds (53%)**
I haven't bet on many Spurs props this year for one reason: they have been blowing the doors off teams, and their starters haven't had to play big minutes all that often. Castle is averaging only 29 minutes per game this year, and San Antonio has spread out the statistical production among all three of their excellent young guards, while [Victor Wembanyama](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003031/Victor+Wembanyama) does a lot of the heavy lifting, too.
Castle and [Dylan Harper](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003445/Dylan+Harper) are both very good rebounders at their positions, and because the Spurs play very small with basically four guards ([Devin Vassell](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002536/Devin+Vassell) is a 6-foot-five shooting guard starting at the four), whichever boards that Wemby doesn't snag are up for grabs. Castle has at least five boards in 8 of their 10 games, and he's gone for 21 points and rebounds now in 7 of 10.
The spread is 8.5 here, so perhaps the Suns, who have been playing a bit better, can actually make a game of this. Because if we get 30 minutes or more from Castle, he'll crush this number.
**[Stephen Curry](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20000485/Stephen+Curry) OVER 28.5 Points (51%)**
It's been a lot of fun to see Steph back doing Steph things this year. He's absolutely on fire now, averaging 35.8 points per game over his last five and going over this number in all five.
The only concern here is that the Pelicans are on a back-to-back and are 9.5-point underdogs. They'll need to put up enough of a fight to get some fourth-quarter minutes for Steph. But to be fair, he had 29 in only 28 minutes against the Heat the other night. He's locked in (as the kids say) and has drilled at least five threes in all five of those games, too (his three-point prop looks juicy, too).
Oh, and did I mention that the Pelicans are allowing the most points to opposing point guards in the league?

## NBA Value Props
_All odds are from Novig and were accurate as of 6:00 AM ET. All odds listed are from Novig and shown as percentages, not traditional American odds._
**[CJ McCollum](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20000620/CJ+McCollum) OVER 12.5 Points (51%)**
McCollum has moved to the bench for the last two games, and that's where I expect him to remain going forward. So why do we like his points prop, if he just got demoted?
Well, his prop number goes down, but his usage has actually gone up. He's been at 23% and 30% usage in his two games coming off the bench; he's just shot it terribly (1-for-10 and then 4-for-14).
He won't shoot that badly forever, and he only needs to hit five or six shots to cash this prop. Even if we project his minutes to stay in the 21-22 range, he's putting up enough shots to get there tonight, especially in what should be a high-paced, high-scoring game against the Lakers (who are on the second half of a back-to-back).
**[Walker Kessler](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002889/Walker+Kessler) OVER 24.5 Points + Rebounds (50%)**
Kessler is averaging 28 points and boards over his last five games. This game is the tightest spread of the night (1.5 points) and has the highest total (236.5 points) on the slate, so I expect to see Kessler in his normal allotment of minutes, even on a back-to-back.
Atlanta is allowing the third-most points per possession on putbacks and the fourth-most to roll men on the pick-and-roll. That's pretty much how Kessler derives most of his scoring, so this matchup plays right into his strengths.
**[Cedric Coward](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003477/Cedric+Coward) OVER 6.5 Rebounds (51%) **
Some bettors simply won't bet on a number that hasn't missed yet, with the logic that the law of averages suggests that regression is bound to come and the next game could be the one where the bubble bursts.
But I'm still hammering Coward's rebounding prop today; I don't care that he hasn't gone under 7 rebounds yet this season!
I always want to look at the context surrounding the player's performance. Coward's rebounding this season (8.8 per game in 28.5 minutes per game) might look fluky to some, but I think it's a product of a few things. First of all, [Zach Edey](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003199/Zach+Edey) is still only playing around 20 minutes a night, and so anytime that Coward is on the floor when Edey isn't, he's instantly one of the top two rebounders on the team, along with rookie [Cameron Boozer](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20003709/Cameron+Boozer).
Toronto isn't a great matchup for offensive stats, but I this should be a really good game that stays close, and you could argue that Toronto's very good defense is going to create more offensive rebounding opportunities, too. Coward's length from the wing gives him an advantage over smaller guards, and he's not afraid to mix it up with the big men in the paint either.
**[Obi Toppin](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20002533/Obi+Toppin) OVER 15.5 Points + Rebounds (52%)**
You want another prop that hasn't missed yet this season? Yep, Obi is a perfect 7-for-7 on this number and should be line for 20+ minutes again tonight for the Pacers as [Ivica Zubac](https://www.rotoballer.com/nba/player/20001694/Ivica+Zubac) is still on a minutes restriction.
The Mavs give up a lot of boards and points to big men, which I think we can classify Toppin as a small-ball center. He's also firing away from deep, averaging five three-point attempts per game. This game is definitely one of my favorites for props tonight; I think it stays close and produces a lot of stats!
Good luck if you're tailing, and thanks for reading!
